WESTERN DAILY MERCURY: THURSDAY, FRIDAY & 9 AUGUST 1895-2

LEFT COLUMN:

RAGLAN BARRACKS v. NORTH DEVON.
This match was played at Mount Wise yesterday afternoon. The Raglan Barracks team was composed of members of the Somerset Light Infantry and Berkshire Regiment. The Barracks went in first on a comparatively easy wicket, and knocked up 107. Largely the visitors knocked up 113 for six wickets, thus securing a substantial win. Score:—

RAGLAN BARRACKS.
Sir Thomas Paddy (Berkshire Regiment), c b Johnson
Sergeant Naylor (Somerset Light Infantry), b Slater
Burjer Ward (Berkshire Light Infantry), b Johnson
Carbain Swayne (Somerset Light Infantry), b Slater
Lance-Corporal Barrett (Berkshire Regiment) ............ 41

TAVISTOCK v. PLYMOUTH.
Played at Tavistock on Tuesday. Plymouth began with-side Cosey and Collins, putting on 26 for the first wicket, not against the bowling of Wood and Milford the others only added 53, and the innings closed for 135. Tavistock started badly, losing Penry without scoring. Moore (pro), although he had a little bit of luck, played a sound game, further parting him to be one of Devon's best bats at the present, finished not out 130, the home team winning a good game by 30 runs. Score:—

TAVISTOCK.
Cosey, b W. b Wood ....

PLYMOUTH POLICE v. SHIRWILL WEDNESDAY.
Played at South Devon-place yesterday, a pleasant game resulting in a win for the Police by 6 runs on the first innings. Collins top scored with 32, Burland well, Herring making N.E. down, 7 maidens, 14 runs, 6 wickets. Score:—

POLICE.
Furner, b Cunnine
Densam, b Dibley
Davis, c Matthews, b Sibley
